(function () {
"use strict";
var S = { me: null, csrf: null, cleanups: [], offline: false };
var EDITOR_URL = "/editor.__AL_EDITOR_HASH16__.js";
var FRAME_SANDBOX = __AL_FRAME_SANDBOX__;
// Lazy-load the syntax-highlighting editor once per page; resolves null offline or on any failure
// (callers keep the plain textarea). Same-origin script, allowed by script-src 'self'.
var editorP = null;
function loadEditor() {
  if (S.offline || !navigator.onLine) return Promise.resolve(null);
  if (window.AL_EDITOR && window.AL_EDITOR.mountEditor) return Promise.resolve(window.AL_EDITOR);
  if (!editorP) editorP = new Promise(function (res) {
    var sc = document.createElement("script");
    sc.src = EDITOR_URL; sc.async = true;
    sc.onload = function () { res(window.AL_EDITOR && window.AL_EDITOR.mountEditor ? window.AL_EDITOR : null); };
    sc.onerror = function () { editorP = null; res(null); };
    document.head.appendChild(sc);
  });
  return editorP;
}
// The document editor currently on screen (CodeMirror adapter or plain textarea), for resume/caret logic.
var activeEd = null;
// Read-only accessor for browser tests and debugging (returns the same object the app uses; no new capability).
window.__alActiveEditor = function () { return activeEditor(); };
function activeEditor() {
  if (activeEd && activeEd.dom && document.contains(activeEd.dom)) return activeEd;
  var ta = document.querySelector("textarea.editor");
  return ta && !ta.hidden ? ta : null;
}
/*__AL_HOME_JS__*/

var crumbsEl = document.getElementById("crumbs");
var whoEl = document.getElementById("who");
var navEl = document.getElementById("nav");

(function brandMark() {
  var url = null;
  try {
    var bin = atob(FOLD_MARK_PNG), u8 = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
    for (var i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) u8[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([u8], { type: "image/png" }));
    document.querySelectorAll(".logo").forEach(function (el) {
      var img = document.createElement("img"); img.src = url; img.alt = ""; img.width = 28; img.height = 28; img.decoding = "async";
      el.replaceChildren(img); el.dataset.logo = "fold";
    });
  } catch (e) { if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url); } // the "A" fallback tile stays if anything fails
})();

// ---------- DOM helpers (text only, never innerHTML) ----------
function h(tag, attrs) {
  var el = document.createElement(tag);
  if (attrs) for (var k in attrs) {
    var v = attrs[k];
    if (v === null || v === undefined || v === false) continue;
    if (k.slice(0, 2) === "on") el.addEventListener(k.slice(2), v);
    else if (k === "class") el.className = v;
    else if (k === "text") el.textContent = v;
    else if (k === "dataset") { for (var d in v) el.dataset[d] = v[d]; }
    else if (k in el && k !== "list" && k !== "form") { try { el[k] = v; } catch (e) { el.setAttribute(k, v); } }
    else el.setAttribute(k, v === true ? "" : String(v));
  }
  for (var i = 2; i < arguments.length; i++) add(el, arguments[i]);
  return el;
}
function add(el, c) {
  if (c === null || c === undefined || c === false) return;
  if (Array.isArray(c)) { c.forEach(function (x) { add(el, x); }); return; }
  el.appendChild(typeof c === "object" ? c : document.createTextNode(String(c)));
}
function enc(s) { return encodeURIComponent(s); }
// In-page view switchers use the WAI-ARIA tabs pattern: one tab stop (roving tabindex), arrows/Home/End move between tabs.
// Route-changing switchers (project and connection sections) are plain links with aria-current instead.
var tabSeq = 0;
function tabList(label) {
  var nav = h("div", { class: "tabs", role: "tablist", "aria-label": label });
  nav.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
    var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(nav.querySelectorAll("[role=tab]")), i = tabs.indexOf(document.activeElement);
    if (i < 0) return;
    var j = e.key === "ArrowRight" ? (i + 1) % tabs.length : e.key === "ArrowLeft" ? (i - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length : e.key === "Home" ? 0 : e.key === "End" ? tabs.length - 1 : -1;
    if (j < 0) return;
    e.preventDefault(); tabs[j].focus(); tabs[j].click();
  });
  return nav;
}
function tabButton(label, panel, onclick) {
  var id = "tab-" + (++tabSeq), b = h("button", { type: "button", role: "tab", id: id, "aria-selected": "false", tabindex: "-1", onclick: onclick }, label);
  if (panel) { if (!panel.id) panel.id = "panel-" + tabSeq; panel.setAttribute("role", "tabpanel"); panel.setAttribute("aria-labelledby", id); b.setAttribute("aria-controls", panel.id); }
  return b;
}
function selectTab(nav, btn) {
  nav.querySelectorAll("[role=tab]").forEach(function (b) { var on = b === btn; b.setAttribute("aria-selected", on ? "true" : "false"); b.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1; });
}
function linkTabs(label, tabs, active) {
  return h("nav", { class: "tabs", "aria-label": label }, tabs.map(function (t) {
    return h("a", { href: t[2], "aria-current": t[0] === active ? "page" : null }, t[1]);
  }));
}

function icon(name) {
  var NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  var svg = document.createElementNS(NS, "svg"); svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 24 24"); svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  (ICONS[name] || []).forEach(function (d) { var p = document.createElementNS(NS, "path"); p.setAttribute("d", d); svg.appendChild(p); });
  return svg;
}
function iconButton(name, label, onclick, extra) {
  return h("button", Object.assign({ type: "button", class: "icon", title: label, "aria-label": label, onclick: onclick }, extra || {}), icon(name));
}
function iconLink(name, label, href, extra) {
  return h("a", Object.assign({ class: "icon", href: href, title: label, "aria-label": label }, extra || {}), icon(name));
}
var toastTimer = null;
function toast(text) {
  var t = document.querySelector(".toast") || document.body.appendChild(h("div", { class: "toast", role: "status", "aria-live": "polite" }));
  t.textContent = text; t.hidden = false;
  clearTimeout(toastTimer); toastTimer = setTimeout(function () { t.hidden = true; }, 1800);
}
// Copy with a brief checkmark on the button and a toast.
function copyIcon(name, label, get) {
  var b = iconButton(name, label, function () {
    var v = get();
    var ok = function () { b.classList.add("done"); b.replaceChildren(icon("check")); toast(label.replace(/^Copy /, "") + " copied");
      setTimeout(function () { b.classList.remove("done"); b.replaceChildren(icon(name)); }, 1400); };
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(v).then(ok, function () { toast("Copy failed"); });
    else toast("Clipboard unavailable");
  });
  return b;
}
function uuid() {
  if (crypto.randomUUID) return crypto.randomUUID();
  var b = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  return Array.from(b, function (x) { return x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"); }).join("");
}
function hex(buf) { return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf), function (x) { return x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"); }).join(""); }
function fmtDate(s) { if (!s) return ""; var d = new Date(typeof s === "number" ? s : s); return isNaN(d) ? String(s) : d.toLocaleString(); }
function fmtBytes(n) { if (n == null) return ""; if (n < 1024) return n + " B"; if (n < 1048576) return (n / 1024).toFixed(1) + " KB"; if (n < 1073741824) return (n / 1048576).toFixed(1) + " MB"; return (n / 1073741824).toFixed(1) + " GB"; }
// .msg elements are live regions: errors (showErr) are alerts, everything else is a polite status.
function showMsg(el, text, ok, isError) {
  el.className = ok ? "msg ok" : "msg"; el.removeAttribute("aria-live"); el.setAttribute("role", isError ? "alert" : "status");
  el.textContent = text || "";
}
function showErr(el, e) {
  var t = e && e.message ? e.message : String(e);
  // Plain-language refusals (limits) speak for themselves; other errors keep the status for support.
  if (e && e.status && e.code !== "limit_reached") t = t + " (" + e.status + (e.code ? " " + e.code : "") + ")";
  showMsg(el, t, false, true);
}
function copyText(text, msgEl) {
  var done = function () { if (msgEl) showMsg(msgEl, "Copied to clipboard.", true); };
  if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, function () { if (msgEl) showMsg(msgEl, "Copy failed; select the text and copy manually."); });
  else if (msgEl) showMsg(msgEl, "Clipboard unavailable; select the text and copy manually.");
}

// ---------- API ----------
function ApiError(status, data) {
  var err = (data && data.error) || {};
  this.status = status; this.data = data; this.code = err.code || null;
  this.message = err.message || ("HTTP " + status);
}
ApiError.prototype = Object.create(Error.prototype);

async function api(method, path, body, opt) {
  opt = opt || {};
  var headers = Object.assign({}, opt.headers || {});
  if (body !== undefined && !opt.raw) headers["content-type"] = "application/json";
  if (method !== "GET" && method !== "HEAD" && S.csrf) headers["x-csrf-token"] = S.csrf;
  if (opt.key) headers["idempotency-key"] = opt.key;
  headers["x-requested-with"] = "XMLHttpRequest";
  var res;
  try {
    res = await fetch(path, { method: method, headers: headers, credentials: "same-origin", cache: "no-store",
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : (opt.raw ? body : JSON.stringify(body)) });
  } catch (e) { throw new ApiError(0, { error: { code: "network", message: "Network error: " + (e && e.message) } }); }
  var data = null, t = await res.text();
  if (t) { try { data = JSON.parse(t); } catch (e) { data = { raw: t }; } }
  if (res.status === 401 && !opt.noAuthRedirect) {
    if (!opt.renewed && await renewSession()) return api(method, path, body, Object.assign({}, opt, { renewed: true }));
    onUnauthenticated();
  }
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(res.status, data);
  return data;
}

// ---------- hosted session renewal ----------
// Hosted = behind Cloudflare Access; loopback (localhost/127.0.0.1) keeps the local dev passphrase login.
// Installations with redirect sign-in (GET /auth/methods redirect:true, e.g. hosted Supabase) sign in at /auth/signin.
function hostedApp() { return !/^(localhost|127\.0\.0\.1)$/.test(location.hostname); }
var AUTH = null;
async function authMethods() {
  if (AUTH) return AUTH;
  try { var r = await fetch("/auth/methods", { credentials: "same-origin", cache: "no-store" }); AUTH = r.ok ? await r.json() : {}; }
  catch (e) { AUTH = {}; }
  return AUTH;
}
function redirectSignIn() { return !!(AUTH && AUTH.redirect); }
// Loads Cloudflare's Turnstile script once (allowed by the shell's CSP only when the installation enables it) and
// renders the widget into el. The submit button stays disabled until the check passes.
var turnstileLoading = null;
function renderTurnstile(el, siteKey, submit) {
  submit.disabled = true;
  var note = h("p", { class: "small muted", role: "status", text: "Checking that you're human\u2026" });
  el.appendChild(note);
  turnstileLoading = turnstileLoading || new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
    var s = document.createElement("script");
    s.src = "https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js?render=explicit"; s.async = true;
    s.onload = resolve; s.onerror = reject; document.head.appendChild(s);
  });
  turnstileLoading.then(function () {
    note.remove();
    window.turnstile.render(el, { sitekey: siteKey, size: "flexible",
      callback: function () { submit.disabled = false; },
      "expired-callback": function () { submit.disabled = true; },
      "error-callback": function () { submit.disabled = true; el.appendChild(h("p", { class: "small msg", role: "alert", text: "The human check couldn't load. Use a sign-in button above, or reload the page." })); } });
  }).catch(function () { note.textContent = "The human check couldn't load. Use a sign-in button above, or reload the page."; note.setAttribute("role", "alert"); });
}
function signInHref() { var n = location.hash && location.hash !== "#/" ? location.hash : ""; return "/auth/signin" + (n ? "?next=" + encodeURIComponent(n) : ""); }
var renewing = null, signingOut = false;
// The app session ended but Access may still be signed in: get a new app session for the same user.
function renewSession() {
  if (!hostedApp() || redirectSignIn() || !S.me || signingOut) return Promise.resolve(false);
  if (!renewing) {
    var uid = S.me.id;
    renewing = (async function () {
      try {
        await api("POST", "/auth/access-login", {}, { noAuthRedirect: true });
        var me = await api("GET", "/api/me", undefined, { noAuthRedirect: true });
        if (!S.me || S.me.id !== uid || !me.principal || me.principal.id !== uid) return false;
        S.csrf = me.csrf; return true;
      } catch (e) { return false; }
    })().finally(function () { renewing = null; });
  }
  return renewing;
}
// Access itself expired (or renewal failed): reload so Access shows its sign-in page. At most once a minute,
// so a server-side refusal cannot cause a reload loop.
function reloadForSignIn() {
  try {
    var last = Number(sessionStorage.getItem("al:signin-reload") || 0);
    if (Date.now() - last < 60000) return false;
    sessionStorage.setItem("al:signin-reload", String(Date.now()));
  } catch (e) { return false; }
  location.reload();
  return true;
}

// ---------- drafts (scoped by principal/project/artifact; cleared on logout/401) ----------
function draftKey(pid, aid) { return "al:draft:" + (S.me ? S.me.id : "anon") + ":" + pid + ":" + aid; }
function readDraft(pid, aid) { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(draftKey(pid, aid)) || "null"); } catch (e) { return null; } }
function writeDraft(pid, aid, base, body) { try { localStorage.setItem(draftKey(pid, aid), JSON.stringify({ base: base, body: body, at: Date.now() })); } catch (e) {} }
function clearDraft(pid, aid) { try { localStorage.removeItem(draftKey(pid, aid)); } catch (e) {} }
function clearAllDrafts() {
  try { for (var i = localStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var k = localStorage.key(i); if (k && k.indexOf("al:draft:") === 0) localStorage.removeItem(k); } } catch (e) {}
}

// Drop drafts that belong to any principal other than the one now signed in (identity isolation).
function purgeForeignDrafts(principalId) {
  HOME.cacheClear(principalId); thumbClear();
  var mine = "al:draft:" + principalId + ":";
  try { for (var i = localStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var k = localStorage.key(i); if (k && k.indexOf("al:draft:") === 0 && k.indexOf(mine) !== 0) localStorage.removeItem(k); } } catch (e) {}
}
// Session ended (expiry/401): unsaved text is NOT destroyed. Drafts are keyed by principal, are already
// persisted on every keystroke, and are only re-offered to the same principal after sign-in. Explicit
// logout still clears all drafts; a different principal signing in purges them.
function onUnauthenticated() {
  var wasIn = !!S.me;
  HOME.cacheClear(null); thumbClear();
  S.me = null; S.csrf = null;
  runCleanups(true);
  if (wasIn && hostedApp() && !redirectSignIn() && !signingOut && reloadForSignIn()) return;
  viewLogin(wasIn ? "Your session ended. Any unsaved text was kept in this browser and will be offered again after you sign in as the same user. Log out to clear it." : "");
}

// ---------- shell / routing ----------
function runCleanups(discard) { var c = S.cleanups; S.cleanups = []; c.forEach(function (f) { try { f(discard); } catch (e) {} }); }
function setCrumbs(items) {
  crumbsEl.replaceChildren();
  items.forEach(function (it, i) {
    if (i) crumbsEl.append(h("span", { class: "sep", "aria-hidden": "true", text: "/" }));
    var last = i === items.length - 1;
    // The current page is plain text (not a link); earlier crumbs stay navigable.
    crumbsEl.append(it.href && !last ? h("a", { href: it.href, text: it.t, title: it.t }) : h("span", { text: it.t, title: it.t, "aria-current": last ? "page" : null }));
  });
}
// ---------- primary navigation (sidebar on desktop, bottom tab bar on mobile) ----------
var NAV = [["home", "Home", "#/", "home"], ["projects", "Projects", "#/projects", "folder"], ["workspaces", "Workspaces", "#/workspaces", "users", "Spaces"], ["connections", "Connections", "#/settings/apps", "plug", "Apps"], ["settings", "Settings", "#/settings/device", "settings"]];
function navSection() {
  var p = location.hash.replace(/^#\/?/, "").split("/").filter(Boolean);
  if (p[0] === "projects" || p[0] === "p") return "projects";
  if (p[0] === "workspaces" || p[0] === "invite") return "workspaces";
  if (p[0] === "apps" || (p[0] === "settings" && ["apps", "publishing", "api-keys", "tokens"].indexOf(p[1]) >= 0)) return "connections";
  if (p[0] === "settings") return "settings";
  return "home";
}
function setNav() {
  var signedIn = !!S.me && !S.offline;
  document.body.classList.toggle("signed-out", !signedIn);
  navEl.replaceChildren();
  if (!signedIn) return;
  var cur = navSection();
  NAV.forEach(function (n) {
    // Single-user installations (self-host behind Access) have no shared workspaces: no Workspaces section.
    if (n[0] === "workspaces" && !S.multiUser) return;
    navEl.append(h("a", { href: n[2], "aria-current": n[0] === cur ? "page" : null, dataset: { nav: n[0] } }, icon(n[3]), h("span", { class: "full", text: n[1] }), n[4] ? h("span", { class: "short", text: n[4] }) : null));
  });
}
function initials(s) { var t = String(s || "?").trim(); return (t[0] || "?").toUpperCase(); }
function setWho() {
  whoEl.replaceChildren();
  if (!S.me) return;
  var name = S.me.label || S.me.id;
  var menu = h("div", { class: "menu", role: "menu", hidden: true },
    h("div", { class: "who-label" }, "Signed in to Agent Library as", h("strong", { text: name })),
    h("a", { href: "#/settings/apps", role: "menuitem", onclick: closeMenu }, icon("plug"), "Connected apps"),
    h("a", { href: "#/settings/api-keys", role: "menuitem", onclick: closeMenu }, icon("key"), "API keys"),
    h("a", { href: "#/settings/tokens", role: "menuitem", onclick: closeMenu }, icon("key"), "Agent tokens"),
    h("a", { href: "#/settings/device", role: "menuitem", onclick: closeMenu }, icon("phone"), "Offline on this device"),
    h("hr", { role: "separator" }),
    h("button", { type: "button", role: "menuitem", onclick: function () { closeMenu(); logout(); } }, icon("logout"), "Sign out"));
  var btn = h("button", { type: "button", class: "acct", "aria-label": "Account: " + name, "aria-haspopup": "menu", "aria-expanded": "false", title: "Account and settings",
    onclick: function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); menu.hidden ? openMenu() : closeMenu(); } },
    h("span", { class: "avatar", "aria-hidden": "true", text: initials(name) }), h("span", { class: "acct-name", text: name }));
  function items() { return Array.prototype.slice.call(menu.querySelectorAll("a,button")); }
  function onDoc(e) { if (!menu.contains(e.target)) closeMenu(); }
  function onKey(e) {
    if (e.key === "Escape") { closeMenu(); btn.focus(); return; }
    if (e.key === "Tab") { closeMenu(); return; }
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown" || e.key === "ArrowUp") {
      e.preventDefault(); var xs = items(), i = xs.indexOf(document.activeElement);
      xs[(i + (e.key === "ArrowDown" ? 1 : xs.length - 1)) % xs.length].focus();
    }
  }
  function openMenu() { menu.hidden = false; btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true"); document.addEventListener("click", onDoc); document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey); var f = items()[0]; if (f) f.focus(); }
  function closeMenu() { menu.hidden = true; btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false"); document.removeEventListener("click", onDoc); document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey); }
  whoEl.append(btn, menu);
}
function shell(crumbs) {
  setCrumbs(crumbs || []); setWho(); setNav();
  app.replaceChildren();
  for (var i = 1; i < arguments.length; i++) add(app, arguments[i]);
  applyRouteFocus();
}
// Page header: optional eyebrow, title (h1), optional subtitle; actions on the right.
function pageHead(title, opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  var h1 = typeof title === "string" ? h("h1", { text: title }) : title;
  return h("div", { class: "page-head" },
    h("div", { class: "ph-text" }, opts.eyebrow ? h("span", { class: "eyebrow", text: opts.eyebrow }) : null, h1, opts.sub ? h("p", { class: "ph-sub", text: opts.sub }) : null),
    opts.actions ? h("div", { class: "ph-actions" }, opts.actions) : null);
}
// Purposeful empty/error state with a supported next step.
function stateBox(kind, iconName, title, text, actions) {
  return h("div", { class: "state" + (kind === "error" ? " error" : ""), role: kind === "error" ? "alert" : null },
    h("span", { class: "st-icon", "aria-hidden": "true" }, icon(iconName)), h("h3", { text: title }), text ? h("p", { text: text }) : null,
    actions ? h("div", { class: "row" }, actions) : null);
}
// Loading placeholder rows that reserve layout space.
function skeletonRows(list, n) {
  list.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
  list.replaceChildren(h("li", { class: "sr", text: "Loading…" }));
  for (var i = 0; i < (n || 3); i++) list.append(h("li", { class: "skel", "aria-hidden": "true" }, h("div", { class: "grow" }, h("span", { class: "skel-line wide" }), h("span", { class: "skel-line" }))));
}
function doneLoading(list) { list.removeAttribute("aria-busy"); }
function rowLink(href, title) { return h("a", { class: "stretch", href: href, text: title }); }
function kindIcon(a) { var t = HOME.typeOf(a); return t === "image" ? "image" : t === "video" ? "video" : t === "csv" ? "table" : t === "json" ? "braces" : t === "file" ? "file" : "doc"; }
// Move focus to the new page content after in-app navigation, so keyboard and screen-reader users land on it.
// Decided when the route changes; applied after the new view has replaced main's content (shell), so a control
// that was inside the old view (tab, document row, Load more…) can't leave focus stranded on <body>.
// Not applied on first load (no navigation happened) or when focus is in the header/sidebar and still attached.
var routedOnce = false, focusAfterRender = false;
function focusMain() { focusAfterRender = routedOnce; routedOnce = true; }
function applyRouteFocus() {
  if (!focusAfterRender) return;
  var a = document.activeElement;
  // Something in the new view already took focus deliberately (e.g. sign-in field, conflict editor): keep it.
  if (a && a !== document.body && app.contains(a) && a !== app) { focusAfterRender = false; return; }
  // Focus left on a still-attached header/sidebar control (breadcrumb, nav link, account menu) moves to the content too,
  // so keyboard and screen-reader users land on the new page.
  focusAfterRender = false;
  try { app.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {}
}
var projectTitles = {};
// Effective access per project from /api/projects (role in its workspace). The server enforces it; the UI only
// hides controls a viewer can't use. Unknown = treat as writable (never hide on missing data).
var projectAccess = {};
// Project addresses (<workspace handle>/<project slug>) from /api/projects, for links people copy and share.
var projectPaths = {};
function noteProjects(list) { (list || []).forEach(function (p) { if (p.path) projectPaths[p.id] = p.path; projectTitles[p.id] = p.title; projectAccess[p.id] = { role: p.role || "owner", can_write: p.can_write !== false, workspace_id: p.workspace_id, read_only_reason: p.read_only_reason || null }; }); }
function canWrite(pid) { var a = projectAccess[pid]; return !a || a.can_write; }
async function ensureProjectAccess(pid) {
  if (projectAccess[pid]) return projectAccess[pid];
  try { noteProjects((await api("GET", "/api/projects")).projects); } catch (e) {}
  return projectAccess[pid] || null;
}
// Unsaved text from before access became view-only: readable, copyable, discardable; never saved.
function unsavedDraftBox(text, onDiscard) {
  var msg = h("p", { class: "msg", role: "status" });
  var area = h("textarea", { class: "draft-text", readOnly: true, rows: 6, "aria-label": "Your unsaved text (not saved)" }); area.value = text;
  var box = h("div", { class: "card unsaved-draft", role: "alert" }, h("strong", { text: "Your unsaved changes weren't saved" }),
    h("p", { class: "muted small", text: "You now have view-only access. Copy your text to keep it." }), area,
    h("div", { class: "row" },
      h("button", { type: "button", onclick: function () { copyText(text, msg); } }, "Copy text"),
      h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", onclick: function () { if (confirm("Discard your unsaved text?")) { onDiscard(); box.remove(); } } }, "Discard")), msg);
  return box;
}
// For people with view-only access: remove write controls (marked data-write) and say why, once per view.
function viewOnlyNote(pid) {
  var a = pid ? projectAccess[pid] : null;
  var suspended = a && a.read_only_reason === "workspace_suspended";
  return h("div", { class: "callout view-only-note", role: "note" }, icon("alert"), h("div", null, suspended
    ? "This workspace is suspended, so it's read-only. You can still read, download and export everything; an owner can reactivate it."
    : "You have view-only access to this project. Ask a workspace admin if you need to edit."));
}
function projCrumbs(pid, extra) {
  // The brand returns Home; project crumbs keep the existing project navigation.
  return [{ t: projectTitles[pid] || pid, href: "#/p/" + enc(pid) }].concat(extra || []);
}

function route() {
  runCleanups(false);
  var t = document.querySelector(".toast"); if (t) t.hidden = true;
  focusMain();
  var p = location.hash.replace(/^#\/?/, "").replace(/\?.*$/, "").split("/").filter(Boolean).map(decodeURIComponent);
  if (S.offline) return offlineRoute();
  if (!S.me) { var n = S.note || ""; S.note = ""; return viewLogin(n); }
  if (p[0] === "settings" && p[1] === "publishing") return viewPublishingApps();
  if (p[0] === "settings" && p[1] === "device") return viewDevice();
  if (p[0] === "apps" || (p[0] === "settings" && p[1] === "apps")) return viewApps();
  if (p[0] === "settings" && p[1] === "tokens") return viewTokens();
  if (p[0] === "settings" && p[1] === "api-keys") return viewApiKeys();
  if (p[0] === "p" && p[1] && p[1].indexOf("/") > 0) return resolvePathRoute(p);
  if (p[0] === "p" && p[1]) {
    if (p[2] === "a" && p[3]) return viewArtifact(p[1], p[3], p[4] === "edit" ? "edit" : "preview");
    if (p[2] === "trash") return viewList(p[1], true);
    if (p[2] === "grants") { location.replace("#/settings/tokens"); return; }
    return viewList(p[1], false);
  }
  if (p[0] === "search") return viewSearch();
  if (p[0] === "projects") return viewProjects();
  if (p[0] === "workspaces" && !S.multiUser) { location.replace("#/projects"); return; }
  if (p[0] === "workspaces" && p[1]) return viewWorkspace(p[1]);
  if (p[0] === "workspaces") return viewWorkspaces();
  if (p[0] === "invite") return viewInvite(p[1] || "");
  return viewHome();
}

// "#/p/<workspace>%2F<project>/…" (from an address link): find the project's id among the projects this person can
// open, then continue on the id route (replacing the history entry). The link itself grants nothing.
async function resolvePathRoute(p) {
  var want = p[1], token = ++pathSeq;
  var find = function () { for (var id in projectPaths) if (projectPaths[id] === want) return id; return null; };
  // Always ask the server: addresses can change, so a cached match could open the wrong project.
  shell([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Opening…" }], h("div", { role: "status" }, h("span", { class: "sr", text: "Opening project…" }), h("span", { class: "skel-line wide", "aria-hidden": "true" })));
  try { var fresh = (await api("GET", "/api/projects")).projects; projectPaths = {}; noteProjects(fresh); }
  catch (e) {
    if (e.status === 401 || token !== pathSeq) return;
    shell([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Couldn't open" }], stateBox("error", "alert", "Couldn't open this link",
      (e.status === 0 ? "You appear to be offline." : e.message) + " Nothing was changed.",
      h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", onclick: function () { route(); } }, icon("refresh"), "Try again")));
    return;
  }
  if (token !== pathSeq) return;
  var id = find();
  if (!id) {
    shell([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Not available" }], stateBox("error", "alert", "Project not available",
      "The link may be out of date (project addresses can change), or this account can't open it.", h("a", { class: "btn secondary", href: "#/projects" }, "All projects")));
    return;
  }
  history.replaceState(null, "", "#/p/" + enc(id) + (p.length > 2 ? "/" + p.slice(2).map(enc).join("/") : "") + (location.hash.indexOf("?") >= 0 ? location.hash.slice(location.hash.indexOf("?")) : ""));
  route();
}
var pathSeq = 0;

async function logout() {
  signingOut = true;
  if (renewing) await renewing;
  try { await api("POST", "/api/logout", {}, { noAuthRedirect: true }); } catch (e) {}
  clearAllDrafts();
  HOME.cacheClear(null); thumbClear();
  resumeClear();
  await OFF.clear();
  S.me = null; S.csrf = null;
  runCleanups(true);
  S.note = "Signed out. Local drafts and offline copies were removed from this device.";
  if (location.hash && location.hash !== "#/") location.hash = "#/"; else route();
}

// ---------- login ----------
function viewLogin(note) {
  if (redirectSignIn()) {
    var nextHash = location.hash && location.hash !== "#/" ? location.hash : "";
    var choices = (AUTH && AUTH.choices) || [];
    var first = null, parts = [];
    if (!choices.length) { first = h("a", { class: "btn", href: signInHref() }, "Continue to sign in"); parts.push(first); }
    choices.forEach(function (c) {
      if (c.email) {
        var em = h("input", { type: "email", id: "signin-email", name: "email", required: true, autocomplete: "email", placeholder: "you@example.com",
          pattern: "[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]{2,}", title: "Enter an address like you@example.com" });
        var send = h("button", { type: "submit" }, c.label);
        // Human check (Cloudflare Turnstile) when the installation turns it on; the widget adds cf-turnstile-response.
        var human = AUTH && AUTH.turnstile_site_key ? h("div", { class: "signin-human" }) : null;
        if (human) renderTurnstile(human, AUTH.turnstile_site_key, send);
        // A plain form POST (same origin): the server answers with a redirect, like the provider buttons. One submit only.
        if (parts.length) parts.push(h("div", { class: "signin-or", "aria-hidden": "true" }, "or"));
        parts.push(h("form", { method: "post", action: "/auth/signin", class: "row signin-email", onsubmit: function () { send.disabled = true; send.textContent = "Sending\u2026"; } },
          h("input", { type: "hidden", name: "choice", value: c.id }), h("input", { type: "hidden", name: "next", value: nextHash }),
          h("label", { for: "signin-email", class: "small muted", text: "Email" }), em, human, send));
      } else {
        var b = h("a", { class: "btn secondary signin-choice", href: "/auth/signin?choice=" + encodeURIComponent(c.id) + (nextHash ? "&next=" + encodeURIComponent(nextHash) : "") }, c.label);
        parts.push(b); if (!first) first = b;
      }
    });
    var title = h("h1", { text: "Sign in to Agent Library", tabindex: "-1" });
    shell([{ t: "Sign in" }], note ? h("p", { class: "banner", role: "status", text: note }) : null,
      h("div", { class: "card signin" }, title,
        h("p", { class: "muted small", text: "New here? Signing in creates your account if sign-up is open to you." }), parts));
    // Focus the heading (no ring on an option, which would look pre-selected); Tab reaches the options in order.
    title.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    void first;
    return;
  }
  if (hostedApp()) {
    var again = h("button", { type: "button", onclick: function () { location.reload(); } }, "Sign in");
    shell([{ t: "Sign in" }], note ? h("p", { class: "banner", text: note }) : null,
      h("div", { class: "card" }, h("h1", { text: "Sign in" }), h("p", { class: "muted small", text: "Sign in with your Cloudflare Access account to continue." }), again));
    again.focus();
    return;
  }
  var msg = h("p", { class: "msg", role: "status" });
  var pass = h("input", { type: "password", autocomplete: "current-password", placeholder: "Local dev passphrase", required: true, name: "passphrase" });
  var btn = h("button", { type: "submit" }, "Sign in");
  var form = h("form", { class: "card", onsubmit: async function (e) {
    e.preventDefault(); btn.disabled = true; showMsg(msg, "");
    try {
      var r = await api("POST", "/auth/dev-login", { passphrase: pass.value }, { noAuthRedirect: true });
      pass.value = "";
      S.csrf = r.csrf;
      var me = await api("GET", "/api/me", undefined, { noAuthRedirect: true });
      API_BASE_HINT = me.api_base || "See your installation configuration"; S.me = me.principal; S.csrf = me.csrf || S.csrf; S.multiUser = !!me.multi_user;
      purgeForeignDrafts(S.me.id);
      route();
    } catch (err) { showErr(msg, err); btn.disabled = false; }
  } }, h("h1", { text: "Sign in" }),
    h("p", { class: "muted small", text: "Local development login (loopback only)." }),
    h("div", { class: "row" }, pass, btn), msg);
  shell([{ t: "Sign in" }], note ? h("p", { class: "banner", text: note }) : null, form);
  pass.focus();
}

function searchEntry() {
  var link = h("a", { class: "btn", href: "#/search", hidden: true }, icon("search"), "Search library");
  api("GET", "/api/search/status").then(function (r) { link.hidden = !r.ready; }).catch(function () {});
  return link;
}
// ---------- global ranked search ----------
// ---------- workspaces, members, invitations (docs/workspaces.md) ----------
// Only on multi-user installations (S.multiUser); a self-hosted single-owner installation never shows this section.
var ROLE_LABEL = { owner: "Owner", admin: "Admin", member: "Member", viewer: "Viewer" };
var ROLE_HELP = {
  owner: "Everything an admin can do, plus rename or delete the workspace",
  admin: "Manage members, projects and every agent connection",
  member: "Edit documents and connect agents that can edit",
  viewer: "Read only; agents they connect can only read"
};
var ROLE_ORDER = ["viewer", "member", "admin", "owner"];
function wsLabel(w) { return w.personal ? "Personal" : w.name; }
function sectionMsg() { return h("p", { class: "msg", role: "status", "aria-live": "polite" }); }
// Show an error next to the control that caused it, and bring it into view.
function sectionErr(el, err) { showErr(el, err); try { el.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" }); } catch (e) {} }

async function viewWorkspaces() {
  var msg = sectionMsg();
  var list = h("ul", { class: "list", "aria-label": "Workspaces" });
  var name = h("input", { type: "text", placeholder: "Team name", required: true, maxLength: 80, "aria-label": "New workspace name" });
  var createBtn = h("button", { type: "submit" }, "Create workspace");
  var form = h("form", { class: "card row", hidden: true, "aria-label": "New workspace", onsubmit: async function (e) {
    e.preventDefault(); createBtn.disabled = true; showMsg(msg, "");
    try { var r = await api("POST", "/api/workspaces", { name: name.value.trim() }); toast("Created " + r.name); location.hash = "#/workspaces/" + enc(r.id); }
    catch (err) { sectionErr(msg, err); createBtn.disabled = false; }
  } }, name, createBtn, h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", onclick: function () { form.hidden = true; newBtn.hidden = false; newBtn.focus(); } }, "Cancel"));
  var newBtn = h("button", { type: "button", onclick: function () { form.hidden = false; newBtn.hidden = true; name.focus(); } }, icon("plus"), "New workspace");
  shell([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Workspaces" }], pageHead("Workspaces", { sub: "Your personal workspace is private to you. Create a shared workspace to work on projects with other people.", actions: newBtn }), form, msg, list);
  skeletonRows(list, 2);
  async function load() {
    try {
      var r = await api("GET", "/api/workspaces");
      doneLoading(list); list.replaceChildren();
      r.workspaces.forEach(function (w) {
        list.append(h("li", { class: "link" }, h("span", { class: "ficon-sm", "aria-hidden": "true" }, icon(w.personal ? "user" : "users")),
          h("div", { class: "grow" }, rowLink("#/workspaces/" + enc(w.id), wsLabel(w)),
            h("small", { class: "muted", text: ROLE_LABEL[w.role] + (w.personal ? " · only you" : " · " + w.member_count + (w.member_count === 1 ? " member" : " members")) })),
          w.status !== "active" ? statusBadge(w.status) : null,
          h("span", { class: "chev", "aria-hidden": "true" }, icon("chevron"))));
      });
    } catch (err) {
      doneLoading(list); if (err.status === 401) return;
      list.replaceChildren(h("li", null, stateBox("error", "alert", "Workspaces couldn't load", (err.status === 0 ? "You appear to be offline." : err.message) + " Nothing was changed.",
        h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", onclick: function () { skeletonRows(list, 2); load(); } }, icon("refresh"), "Try again"))));
    }
  }
  load();
}

async function viewWorkspace(id) {
  var body = h("div");
  var loading = h("div", { role: "status" }, h("span", { class: "sr", text: "Loading workspace…" }), h("span", { class: "skel-line wide", "aria-hidden": "true" }), h("span", { class: "skel-line", "aria-hidden": "true" }));
  body.append(loading);
  shell([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Workspaces", href: "#/workspaces" }, { t: "Loading…" }], body);
  var linkBox = h("div", { class: "card", hidden: true }); // kept across reloads while an invitation link is shown
  var focusAfter = null; // selector of the element to focus after the next reload
  async function load() {
    var d, projects, usage;
    try {
      var r = await Promise.all([api("GET", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id) + "/members"), api("GET", "/api/projects"),
        api("GET", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id) + "/usage").catch(function () { return null; })]);
      d = r[0]; noteProjects(r[1].projects); projects = r[1].projects.filter(function (p) { return p.workspace_id === id; }); usage = r[2];
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 401) return;
      setCrumbs([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Workspaces", href: "#/workspaces" }, { t: "Not available" }]);
      body.replaceChildren(stateBox("error", "alert", err.status === 404 ? "Workspace not found" : "Workspace couldn't load",
        err.status === 404 ? "It doesn't exist or you're not a member." : err.message, h("a", { class: "btn secondary", href: "#/workspaces" }, "All workspaces")));
      return;
    }
    var ws = d.workspace, suspended = ws.status === "suspended";
    // Suspended workspaces are read-only: no new projects, invites, role changes or renames (cleanup still works).
    var realAdmin = ws.role === "owner" || ws.role === "admin";
    var admin = !suspended && realAdmin, owner = ws.role === "owner";
    setCrumbs([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Workspaces", href: "#/workspaces" }, { t: wsLabel(ws) }]);
    var owners = d.members.filter(function (m) { return m.role === "owner"; }).length;

    // Projects in this workspace (what members can reach), with a shortcut to create one here.
    var projMsg = sectionMsg();
    var projList = h("ul", { class: "list", "aria-label": "Projects in " + wsLabel(ws) });
    if (!projects.length) projList.append(h("li", { class: "empty", text: admin ? "No projects yet." : "No projects you can open yet." }));
    projects.forEach(function (p) {
      projList.append(h("li", { class: "link" }, h("span", { class: "ficon-sm", "aria-hidden": "true" }, icon("folder")),
        h("div", { class: "grow" }, rowLink("#/p/" + enc(p.id), p.title), h("small", { class: "muted", text: (p.path ? "/" + p.path : "") + (p.role === "viewer" ? (p.path ? " · " : "") + "View only" : "") })),
        h("span", { class: "chev", "aria-hidden": "true" }, icon("chevron"))));
    });
    var newProj = null;
    if (admin) {
      var ptitle = h("input", { type: "text", required: true, maxLength: 200, placeholder: "Project name", "aria-label": "New project name" });
      var pbtn = h("button", { type: "submit" }, "Create project");
      newProj = h("form", { class: "card row", "aria-label": "New project in " + wsLabel(ws), onsubmit: async function (e) {
        e.preventDefault(); pbtn.disabled = true;
        try { var r = await api("POST", "/api/projects", { title: ptitle.value.trim(), workspace_id: id }); projectTitles[r.id] = r.title; location.hash = "#/p/" + enc(r.id); }
        catch (err) { sectionErr(projMsg, err); pbtn.disabled = false; }
      } }, ptitle, pbtn);
      // Import a project from a backup file (new ids; everything is attributed to you).
      var ifile = h("input", { type: "file", accept: "application/json,.json", "aria-label": "Backup file to import" });
      var ibtn = h("button", { type: "submit", class: "secondary" }, icon("upload"), "Import");
      var importForm = h("form", { class: "card import-form", "aria-label": "Import a project into " + wsLabel(ws), onsubmit: async function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var f = ifile.files && ifile.files[0];
        if (!f) { sectionErr(projMsg, { message: "Choose a backup file (.json) first." }); return; }
        if (f.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) { sectionErr(projMsg, { message: "This backup is larger than 2 MB, the import limit for now. Split the project or remove large files first." }); return; }
        ibtn.disabled = true;
        try {
          var backup = JSON.parse(await f.text());
          var r = await api("POST", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id) + "/import", { backup: backup }, { key: uuid() });
          projectTitles[r.project_id] = r.title; toast("Imported “" + r.title + "” (" + r.artifacts + (r.artifacts === 1 ? " item)" : " items)"));
          location.hash = "#/p/" + enc(r.project_id);
        } catch (err) { sectionErr(projMsg, err instanceof SyntaxError ? { message: "That file isn't an Agent Library backup (not valid JSON)." } : err); ibtn.disabled = false; }
      } }, h("strong", { text: "Import a project from a backup" }),
        h("p", { class: "muted small", text: "Creates a new project in this workspace from a backup file downloaded in Settings (up to 2 MB). Everything gets new IDs and is attributed to you." }),
        h("div", { class: "row" }, ifile, ibtn));
      newProj = h("div", null, newProj, importForm);
    }

    // Members. Role changes are a pending choice saved with a button, and promotions to admin/owner are confirmed.
    var memMsg = sectionMsg();
    var members = h("ul", { class: "list", "aria-label": "Members" });
    d.members.forEach(function (m) {
      var who = h("div", { class: "grow" }, h("strong", { text: m.label + (m.me ? " (you)" : "") }), m.email ? h("small", { class: "muted", text: m.email }) : null);
      var li = h("li", { class: "member", dataset: { pid: m.principal_id } }, h("span", { class: "avatar sm", "aria-hidden": "true", text: initials(m.label) }), who);
      var lastOwner = m.role === "owner" && owners < 2;
      // Owners manage every role; admins manage members and viewers. The last owner has no controls (the server
      // refuses too): a workspace needs at least one owner.
      var canManage = !ws.personal && !lastOwner && (owner || (realAdmin && m.role !== "owner" && m.role !== "admin"));
      var canEdit = canManage && !suspended; // role changes need an active workspace; removing works while suspended
      var controls = h("span", { class: "row member-controls" });
      if (canEdit) {
        var sel = h("select", { "aria-label": "Role for " + m.label });
        (owner ? ROLE_ORDER : ["viewer", "member"]).forEach(function (r) { sel.append(h("option", { value: r, text: ROLE_LABEL[r], selected: r === m.role })); });
        var save = h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", hidden: true }, "Save role");
        sel.addEventListener("change", function () { save.hidden = sel.value === m.role; });
        save.addEventListener("click", async function () {
          var next = sel.value;
          var q = next === "owner" ? "Make " + m.label + " an owner? Owners can rename or delete the workspace and remove other owners, including you."
            : next === "admin" ? "Make " + m.label + " an admin? Admins can manage members, projects and every agent connection in this workspace."
            : m.me ? "Change your own role to " + ROLE_LABEL[next] + "? You may lose access to settings here."
            : ROLE_RANK_UI[next] < ROLE_RANK_UI[m.role] ? "Change " + m.label + " to " + ROLE_LABEL[next] + "? Agents they connected lose the extra access on their next request." : null;
          if (q && !confirm(q)) return;
          save.disabled = true; sel.disabled = true;
          try { await api("PATCH", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id) + "/members/" + enc(m.principal_id), { role: next }); toast(m.label + " is now " + ROLE_LABEL[next]); focusAfter = "li.member[data-pid='" + m.principal_id + "'] select"; load(); }
          catch (err) { sectionErr(memMsg, err); sel.value = m.role; save.hidden = true; save.disabled = false; sel.disabled = false; }
        });
        controls.append(sel, save);
      } else controls.append(h("span", { class: "badge" + (m.role === "owner" ? " accent" : ""), text: ROLE_LABEL[m.role], title: lastOwner && !ws.personal ? "A workspace needs at least one owner. Make someone else an owner first." : null }));
      if (!ws.personal && !lastOwner && (m.me || canManage)) controls.append(h("button", { type: "button", class: "danger secondary", onclick: async function (e) {
        var q = m.me ? "Leave " + ws.name + "? You and the agents you connected lose access to its projects right away." : "Remove " + m.label + " from " + ws.name + "? They and the agents they connected lose access to its projects right away.";
        if (!confirm(q)) return;
        e.target.disabled = true;
        try {
          await api("DELETE", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id) + "/members/" + enc(m.principal_id));
          if (m.me) { toast("You left " + ws.name); location.hash = "#/workspaces"; return; }
          toast("Removed " + m.label); focusAfter = "h2#members-h"; load();
        } catch (err) { sectionErr(memMsg, err); e.target.disabled = false; }
      } }, m.me ? "Leave" : "Remove"));
      li.append(controls);
      members.append(li);
    });

    var parts = [pageHead(wsLabel(ws), { eyebrow: ws.personal ? "Personal workspace" : "Shared workspace",
      sub: ws.personal ? "Only you can see projects here." + (S.multiUser ? " Create a shared workspace to invite people." : "") : "Your role: " + ROLE_LABEL[ws.role] + ". " + ROLE_HELP[ws.role] + "." }),
      suspended ? suspendedBanner(owner, usage && usage.billing) : null,
      ws.handle ? h("p", { class: "proj-addr" }, h("span", { class: "muted small", text: "Project links start with" }), h("code", { text: "/" + ws.handle + "/" })) : null,
      usagePanel(usage, admin, realAdmin && suspended),
      h("h2", { id: "projects-h", tabIndex: -1, text: "Projects" }), projMsg, projList, newProj,
      h("h2", { id: "members-h", tabIndex: -1, text: "Members" }), memMsg, members];

    if (admin && !ws.personal) {
      var invMsg = sectionMsg();
      var email = h("input", { type: "email", required: true, maxLength: 254, placeholder: "name@example.com", "aria-label": "Email address", autocomplete: "off" });
      var role = h("select", { "aria-label": "Role" });
      (owner ? ["viewer", "member", "admin"] : ["viewer", "member"]).forEach(function (r) { role.append(h("option", { value: r, text: ROLE_LABEL[r], selected: r === "member" })); });
      var roleHelp = h("small", { class: "muted", id: "invite-role-help", text: ROLE_HELP.member });
      role.setAttribute("aria-describedby", "invite-role-help");
      role.addEventListener("change", function () { roleHelp.textContent = ROLE_HELP[role.value]; });
      var inviteBtn = h("button", { type: "submit" }, "Create invitation");
      var inviteForm = h("form", { class: "card invite-form", "aria-label": "Invite someone", onsubmit: async function (e) {
        e.preventDefault(); inviteBtn.disabled = true; showMsg(invMsg, "");
        try {
          var r = await api("POST", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id) + "/invites", { email: email.value.trim(), role: role.value });
          email.value = ""; inviteBtn.disabled = false;
          inviteLinkBox(linkBox, r, function () { focusAfter = "#invite-email"; load(); });
          load();
        } catch (err) { sectionErr(invMsg, err); inviteBtn.disabled = false; }
      } }, h("label", { class: "field" }, h("span", { text: "Email" }), email), h("label", { class: "field" }, h("span", { text: "Role" }), role, roleHelp), h("div", { class: "invite-submit" }, inviteBtn));
      email.id = "invite-email";
      var pending = h("ul", { class: "list", "aria-label": "Pending invitations" });
      if (!d.invites.length) pending.append(h("li", { class: "empty", text: "No pending invitations." }));
      d.invites.forEach(function (i) {
        pending.append(h("li", null, h("div", { class: "grow" }, h("strong", { text: i.email }), h("small", { class: "muted", text: ROLE_LABEL[i.role] + " · expires " + fmtDate(i.expires_at) })),
          h("button", { type: "button", class: "danger secondary", onclick: async function (e) {
            if (!confirm("Cancel the invitation for " + i.email + "? The link you sent stops working.")) return;
            e.target.disabled = true;
            try { await api("POST", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id) + "/invites/" + enc(i.id) + "/revoke", {}); toast("Invitation cancelled"); focusAfter = "h2#pending-h"; load(); }
            catch (err) { sectionErr(invMsg, err); e.target.disabled = false; }
          } }, "Cancel invitation")));
      });
      parts.push(h("h2", { text: "Invite someone" }), invMsg, inviteForm, linkBox, h("h2", { id: "pending-h", tabIndex: -1, text: "Pending invitations" }), pending);
    }
    if (owner && !suspended) parts.push(handleForm(ws, function () { focusAfter = "#ws-handle"; load(); }));
    if (owner && !ws.personal) {
      var setMsg = sectionMsg();
      var rename = h("input", { type: "text", value: ws.name, maxLength: 80, required: true, "aria-label": "Workspace name" });
      var renameBtn = h("button", { type: "submit", class: "secondary" }, "Rename");
      var confirmName = h("input", { type: "text", autocomplete: "off", "aria-label": "Type the workspace name to confirm", placeholder: ws.name });
      var delBtn = h("button", { type: "submit", class: "danger", disabled: true }, "Delete workspace");
      confirmName.addEventListener("input", function () { delBtn.disabled = confirmName.value.trim() !== ws.name; });
      var nProj = projects.length, nMem = d.members.length;
      parts.push(h("h2", { text: "Workspace settings" }), setMsg,
        suspended ? null : h("form", { class: "card row", "aria-label": "Rename workspace", onsubmit: async function (e) {
          e.preventDefault();
          var v = rename.value.trim(); if (!v || v === ws.name) return;
          renameBtn.disabled = true;
          try { await api("PATCH", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id), { name: v }); toast("Renamed to " + v); load(); } catch (err) { sectionErr(setMsg, err); renameBtn.disabled = false; }
        } }, h("label", { class: "field grow" }, h("span", { text: "Name" }), rename), renameBtn),
        h("form", { class: "card danger-zone", "aria-label": "Delete workspace", onsubmit: async function (e) {
          e.preventDefault(); if (confirmName.value.trim() !== ws.name) return;
          delBtn.disabled = true;
          try { await api("DELETE", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(id)); toast("Deleted " + ws.name); location.hash = "#/workspaces"; }
          catch (err) { sectionErr(setMsg, err); delBtn.disabled = false; }
        } },
          h("p", null, h("strong", { text: "Delete workspace" }), h("br"),
            h("span", { class: "muted small", text: "Closes it right away for all " + nMem + (nMem === 1 ? " member" : " members") + " and the agents and API keys they connected. Its " + nProj + (nProj === 1 ? " project is" : " projects are") + " no longer reachable by anyone. Export any project you need first (project backup). This can't be undone from the app." })),
          h("label", { class: "field" }, h("span", { text: "Type " + ws.name + " to confirm" }), confirmName), delBtn));
    }
    body.replaceChildren.apply(body, parts.filter(Boolean));
    if (focusAfter) { var f = body.querySelector(focusAfter) || document.querySelector(focusAfter); focusAfter = null; if (f) f.focus(); }
  }
  load();
}
var ROLE_RANK_UI = { viewer: 1, member: 2, admin: 3, owner: 4 };
// Read-only banner; when billing suspended it (admins only see billing), it says why and offers the payment action.
function suspendedBanner(owner, billing) {
  var why = billing && billing.suspended_reason, msg = h("p", { class: "msg", role: "status" });
  var buttons = why ? billingButtons(billing, msg) : [];
  return h("div", { class: "callout", role: "note" }, icon("alert"), h("div", null,
    h("strong", { text: "This workspace is suspended and read-only. " }),
    why ? why + " " : "",
    "Everyone can still read, download and export its projects, and connected agents can still read. Editing, uploads, new projects, invitations and role changes are paused until it's reactivated." + (owner ? " You can still remove members or delete the workspace." : ""),
    buttons.length ? h("div", { class: "row billing-actions" }, buttons) : null, msg));
}
// Billing (hosted; admins only): plan, payment notice and the installation's checkout/portal actions. Each action is a
// same-origin POST that answers { url } on the payment provider, where the browser goes next.
function billingButtons(b, msg) {
  return (b.actions || []).map(function (a) {
    var btn = h("button", { type: "button", class: a.primary ? "" : "secondary" }, a.label);
    btn.onclick = async function () {
      btn.disabled = true; showMsg(msg, "Opening the payment page…", true);
      try {
        var r = await api("POST", a.path, a.body || {});
        if (!r || typeof r.url !== "string") throw new Error("The payment page couldn't be opened. Try again.");
        location.assign(r.url);
      } catch (err) { showErr(msg, err); btn.disabled = false; }
    };
    // Back from the provider's page (back/forward cache): usable again.
    // (one listener per click, removed after use, so re-renders don't pile them up)
    btn.addEventListener("click", function () { addEventListener("pageshow", function (e) { if (e.persisted) { btn.disabled = false; showMsg(msg, ""); } }, { once: true }); });
    return btn;
  });
}
function billingBlock(b) {
  if (!b) return null;
  var msg = h("p", { class: "msg", role: "status" });
  var plan = String(b.plan || "free");
  var line = "Plan: " + plan.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + plan.slice(1) + (b.status ? " (" + b.status + ")" : "") +
    (b.ends_at ? " · ends " + new Date(b.ends_at).toLocaleDateString() : b.period_end ? " · renews " + new Date(b.period_end).toLocaleDateString() : "");
  // When billing suspended the workspace, the reason and the pay button are in the read-only banner instead.
  var buttons = b.suspended_reason ? [] : billingButtons(b, msg);
  return h("div", { class: "billing" }, h("p", { text: line }),
    b.notice ? h("p", { class: "notice" + (b.notice_tone === "info" ? " info" : ""), text: b.notice }) : null,
    buttons.length ? h("div", { class: "row" }, buttons) : null, msg);
}
// Plan usage (hosted installations with plan limits). Nothing is shown when the workspace has no limits.
var USAGE_ROWS = [["storage_bytes", "Storage"], ["projects", "Projects"], ["members", "Members and invitations"], ["agents", "Connected apps and API keys"]];
function usagePanel(u, canManage, suspendedAdmin) {
  var limited = u && USAGE_ROWS.some(function (r) { return u[r[0]] && u[r[0]].limit != null; });
  if (!u || (!limited && !u.billing)) return null;
  var list = h("dl", { class: "usage" });
  USAGE_ROWS.forEach(function (r) {
    var x = u[r[0]]; if (!x || x.limit == null) return;
    var bytes = r[0] === "storage_bytes", pct = x.limit ? Math.min(100, Math.round(100 * x.used / x.limit)) : 100;
    var txt = bytes ? fmtBytes(x.used) + " of " + fmtBytes(x.limit) : x.used + " of " + x.limit;
    var state = x.used > x.limit ? " · over the limit" : x.used >= x.limit ? " · limit reached" : pct >= 80 ? " · nearly full" : "";
    list.append(h("dt", { text: r[1] }), h("dd", null, h("span", { text: txt + state }),
      h("span", { class: "meter" + (pct >= 100 ? " full" : pct >= 80 ? " near" : ""), "aria-hidden": "true" }, h("span", { style: "width:" + pct + "%" }))));
  });
  var over = USAGE_ROWS.some(function (r) { var x = u[r[0]]; return x && x.limit != null && x.used > x.limit; });
  return h("section", { "aria-labelledby": "usage-h" }, h("h2", { id: "usage-h", text: u.billing ? "Plan and billing" : "Plan usage" }), billingBlock(u.billing), limited ? list : null,
    h("p", { class: "muted small", text: (over ? "Everything over a limit is kept; nothing is removed. " : "At a limit nothing is removed; you just can't add more. ") +
      (canManage ? "To make room, delete what you no longer need (projects, documents, invitations or connected apps)." :
        suspendedAdmin ? "While the workspace is read-only, projects and content can't be added or deleted." : "Only the workspace owner or admins can free up room.") }));
}
var ADDR_HINT = "Lower-case letters, digits and hyphens.";
var focusAddrAfterRender = false; // after saving project settings, focus the (re-rendered) address line
function addrInput(id, value, max, help) {
  var input = h("input", { id: id, type: "text", value: value, maxLength: max, required: true, autocomplete: "off", autocapitalize: "none", spellcheck: false,
    pattern: "[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?", "aria-describedby": help, title: ADDR_HINT });
  input.addEventListener("input", function () {
    var v = input.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_]+/g, "-"); if (v !== input.value) input.value = v;
    input.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
  });
  return input;
}
// Errors about an address (taken, reserved, malformed) belong to its field.
function addrError(input, msg, err) {
  sectionErr(msg, err);
  if (err && (err.status === 400 || err.status === 409)) { input.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true"); input.focus(); input.select(); }
}
// Change a workspace's address (handle). Every project link starting with the old address stops working, so say so.
function handleForm(ws, onDone) {
  var msg = sectionMsg();
  var input = addrInput("ws-handle", ws.handle || "", 39, "ws-handle-help");
  var btn = h("button", { type: "submit", class: "secondary" }, "Change address");
  return h("section", { "aria-labelledby": "ws-addr-h" }, h("h2", { id: "ws-addr-h", text: "Workspace address" }), msg,
    h("form", { class: "card", "aria-label": "Workspace address", onsubmit: async function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var v = input.value.trim().toLowerCase(); if (!v || v === ws.handle) return;
      if (!confirm("Change the address to /" + v + "? Existing links that start with /" + ws.handle + "/ will stop working. Nobody loses access, and connected agents and apps keep working.")) return;
      btn.disabled = true;
      try { var r = await api("PATCH", "/api/workspaces/" + enc(ws.id), { handle: v }); toast("Address changed to /" + r.handle); projectPaths = {}; onDone(); }
      catch (err) { addrError(input, msg, err); btn.disabled = false; }
    } }, h("div", { class: "row" }, h("label", { class: "field grow" }, h("span", null, "Address", h("span", { class: "sr", text: " (project links start with /" + (ws.handle || "") + "/)" })),
      h("span", { class: "addr-input" }, h("span", { class: "muted", "aria-hidden": "true", text: "/" }), input)), btn),
      h("p", { id: "ws-handle-help", class: "muted small", text: ADDR_HINT + " Project links start with this address, so changing it breaks existing links to this workspace's projects." })));
}
// Project settings (admins): title and address (slug). Opened from the project page.
function toggleProjectSettings(pid, opener) {
  var box = app.querySelector(".proj-settings");
  if (box) { box.remove(); opener.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false"); opener.focus(); return; }
  var path = projectPaths[pid] || "", handle = path.split("/")[0], slug = path.split("/")[1] || "";
  var msg = sectionMsg();
  var title = h("input", { id: "proj-title", type: "text", value: projectTitles[pid] || "", maxLength: 200, required: true });
  var sl = addrInput("proj-slug", slug, 64, "proj-slug-help");
  var save = h("button", { type: "submit" }, "Save");
  var close = function () { box.remove(); opener.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false"); opener.focus(); };
  box = h("form", { id: "proj-settings", class: "card proj-settings", "aria-label": "Project settings", onkeydown: function (e) { if (e.key === "Escape") { e.preventDefault(); close(); } }, onsubmit: async function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var b = {}, t = title.value.trim(), s = sl.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (t && t !== projectTitles[pid]) b.title = t;
    if (s && s !== slug) {
      if (!confirm("Change the address to /" + handle + "/" + s + "? Existing links to /" + path + " will stop working. Nobody loses access, and connected agents and apps keep working.")) return;
      b.slug = s;
    }
    if (!Object.keys(b).length) { close(); return; }
    save.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await api("PATCH", "/api/projects/" + enc(pid), b);
      projectTitles[pid] = r.title; if (r.path) projectPaths[pid] = r.path;
      toast("Project updated"); focusAddrAfterRender = true; route();
    } catch (err) { if (b.slug) addrError(sl, msg, err); else sectionErr(msg, err); save.disabled = false; }
  } }, h("h2", { text: "Project settings" }), msg,
    h("label", { class: "field" }, h("span", { text: "Title" }), title),
    h("label", { class: "field" }, h("span", null, "Address", h("span", { class: "sr", text: " (after /" + handle + "/)" })), h("span", { class: "addr-input" }, h("span", { class: "muted", "aria-hidden": "true", text: "/" + handle + "/" }), sl)),
    h("p", { id: "proj-slug-help", class: "muted small", text: ADDR_HINT + " Changing it breaks existing links to this project." }),
    h("div", { class: "row" }, save, h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", onclick: close }, "Cancel")));
  var addr = app.querySelector(".proj-addr"); addr.after(box);
  opener.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true"); title.focus();
}
// Shown once after creating an invitation. Like secretBox: copy can fail (plain HTTP, denied permission).
function inviteLinkBox(box, r, onDone) {
  // Hosted emails the link; the page says whether that worked, and always offers the link too.
  var until = new Date(r.expires_at).toLocaleString(undefined, { dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "short" });
  var rules = "It works once, only for someone signed in as " + r.email + ", until " + until + ". You won't see it again.";
  var first = r.emailed ? "We've emailed this invitation to " + r.email + ". You can also send the link yourself."
    : r.email_failed ? "" : "Send this link to " + r.email + ".";
  var note = h("span", { class: "muted small", id: "invite-link-note" }, first, first ? h("br") : null, rules);
  var failed = r.email_failed ? h("div", { class: "callout warn", role: "alert", style: "margin:8px 0" }, icon("alert"), h("div", { text: "We couldn't email this invitation. Copy the link below and send it yourself." })) : null;
  var link = h("input", { class: "token", type: "text", readOnly: true, value: r.accept_url, "aria-label": "Invitation link", "aria-describedby": "invite-link-note" });
  box.replaceChildren(h("p", null, h("strong", { text: "Invitation for " + r.email + " (" + ROLE_LABEL[r.role] + ")" }), h("br"), failed, note), link,
    h("div", { class: "row" }, h("button", { type: "button", onclick: function () {
      link.select();
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(link.value).then(function () { toast("Link copied"); }, function () { toast("Copy failed; select the link and copy it manually"); });
      else toast("Select the link and copy it manually");
    } }, icon("copy"), "Copy link"),
      h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", onclick: function () { box.hidden = true; box.replaceChildren(); if (onDone) onDone(); } }, "Done")));
  box.hidden = false; link.focus(); link.select();
}

async function viewInvite(token) {
  var msg = sectionMsg();
  var box = h("div", { role: "status" }, h("span", { class: "sr", text: "Loading invitation…" }), h("span", { class: "skel-line wide", "aria-hidden": "true" }));
  shell([{ t: "Invitation" }], box);
  if (!token) { box.replaceChildren(stateBox("error", "alert", "Invitation link incomplete", "Open the full link from the invitation.", null)); return; }
  var pv;
  try { pv = await api("POST", "/api/invites/preview", { token: token }); }
  catch (err) {
    if (err.status === 401) return;
    var used = err.status === 410;
    box.replaceChildren(stateBox("error", "alert", used ? "This invitation can't be used" : "Invitation not found",
      used ? (err.data && err.data.error && err.data.error.code === "workspace_unavailable" ? "The workspace is no longer available." : "It was already used, cancelled or has expired. Ask for a new one.") : "Check that you opened the whole link.",
      h("a", { class: "btn secondary", href: "#/" }, "Go to Home")));
    return;
  }
  var from = pv.inviter ? pv.inviter + " invited you" : "You're invited";
  if (pv.already_role) {
    box.replaceChildren(pageHead(pv.workspace_name, { eyebrow: "Invitation", sub: "You're already a member (" + ROLE_LABEL[pv.already_role] + "). Nothing to do." }),
      h("div", { class: "row" }, h("a", { class: "btn", href: "#/workspaces/" + enc(pv.workspace_id) }, "Open " + pv.workspace_name)));
    return;
  }
  var mismatch = h("div", { class: "callout warn", role: "alert", id: "invite-mismatch" }, icon("alert"), h("div", null,
    "This invitation was sent to a different email address than " + (pv.signed_in_email ? "the one you're signed in with (" + pv.signed_in_email + ")" : "this account has") + ". Sign out, then open the link again and sign in with the invited address. Signing out removes this browser's offline copies and unsaved drafts."));
  var accept = h("button", { type: "button", disabled: !pv.email_matches, "aria-describedby": pv.email_matches ? null : "invite-mismatch", onclick: async function () {
    accept.disabled = true; showMsg(msg, "");
    try { var r = await api("POST", "/api/invites/accept", { token: token }); toast("Joined " + pv.workspace_name); location.replace("#/workspaces/" + enc(r.workspace_id)); }
    catch (err) { showErr(msg, err); accept.disabled = false; }
  } }, "Join " + pv.workspace_name);
  var rows = [pageHead("Join " + pv.workspace_name, { eyebrow: "Invitation", sub: from + " as " + ROLE_LABEL[pv.role] + ": " + ROLE_HELP[pv.role].charAt(0).toLowerCase() + ROLE_HELP[pv.role].slice(1) + "." })];
  if (!pv.email_matches) rows.push(mismatch);
  rows.push(msg, h("div", { class: "row" }, accept,
    pv.email_matches ? h("a", { class: "btn secondary", href: "#/" }, "Not now") : h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", onclick: function () { logout(); } }, icon("logout"), "Sign out")));
  box.removeAttribute("role");
  box.replaceChildren.apply(box, rows);
}

async function viewSearch() {
  var alive = true, seq = 0, cursor = null, active = null;
  S.cleanups.push(function () { alive = false; seq++; });
  shell([{t:"Home",href:"#/"},{t:"Search"}], h("p", {role:"status",text:"Checking search availability…"}));
  try {
    var availability = await api("GET", "/api/search/status");
    if (!alive) return;
    if (!availability.ready) {
      shell([{t:"Home",href:"#/"},{t:"Search"}], stateBox("", "search", "Search is being prepared", "An administrator needs to finish indexing existing documents. Refresh after indexing is complete."));
      return;
    }
  } catch(e) { if (alive) shell([{t:"Search"}], stateBox("error", "alert", "Search unavailable", "Refresh to check again.")); return; }

  var query = h("input", { type: "search", name: "query", required: true, maxLength: 200, placeholder: "Search titles and stored text", "aria-label": "Search library", class: "grow" });
  var project = h("select", { name: "project", "aria-label": "Project filter" }, h("option", { value: "", text: "All projects" }));
  var submit = h("button", { type: "submit" }, icon("search"), "Search");
  var status = h("p", { role: "status", "aria-live": "polite", class: "muted" });
  var list = h("ol", { class: "search-results", "aria-label": "Search results" });
  var more = h("button", { type: "button", class: "secondary", hidden: true, onclick: function () { load(true); } }, "More results");
  var form = h("form", { class: "search-form card", role: "search", onsubmit: function (e) { e.preventDefault(); load(false); } },
    h("label", { class: "sr", htmlFor: "global-search-query", text: "Search library" }), query,
    h("label", { class: "small muted", htmlFor: "global-search-project", text: "Project" }), project, submit);
  query.id = "global-search-query"; project.id = "global-search-project";
  project.addEventListener("change", function () { if (query.value.trim()) load(false); });
  function highlight(text, words) {
    var fragment = document.createDocumentFragment(), terms = words.toLowerCase().match(/[\p{L}\p{N}\p{M}]+/gu) || [];
    if (!terms.length) { fragment.append(document.createTextNode(text)); return fragment; }
    var re = new RegExp("(" + terms.join("|") + ")", "giu"), last = 0, match;
    while ((match = re.exec(text))) { fragment.append(document.createTextNode(text.slice(last, match.index)), h("mark", { text: match[0] })); last = match.index + match[0].length; }
    fragment.append(document.createTextNode(text.slice(last))); return fragment;
  }
  async function load(append) {
    var my = ++seq, requested = append ? active : { query: query.value.trim(), project: project.value };
    if (!requested || !requested.query) return;
    if (!append) { active = requested; cursor = null; list.replaceChildren(); }
    more.hidden = true; submit.disabled = true; list.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true"); status.textContent = "Searching…";
    var params = new URLSearchParams({ query: requested.query });
    if (requested.project) params.set("project_ids", requested.project);
    if (append && cursor) params.set("cursor", cursor);
    try {
      var result = await api("GET", "/api/search?" + params);
      if (!alive || my !== seq) return;
      result.results.forEach(function (r) {
        list.append(h("li", { class: "card search-hit" }, fileThumb({ id: r.artifact_id, kind: r.kind, mime: r.mime, revision: r.revision }, r.project_id),
          h("a", { class: "search-title", href: r.preview_url || r.web_url || "/p/" + enc(r.project_id) + "/a/" + enc(r.artifact_id) + "/preview?v=" + r.revision }, highlight(r.title, requested.query)),
          h("p", { class: "meta", text: r.project_title + " · " + kindLabel(r) + " · Version " + r.revision + " · " + fmtDate(r.updated_at) }),
          h("p", { class: "search-snippet" }, highlight(r.snippet, requested.query)),
          !r.indexed_body ? h("p", { class: "muted small", text: "Title match only · No indexed body" }) : null));
      });
      cursor = result.next_cursor; more.hidden = !result.has_more;
      status.textContent = list.children.length ? list.children.length + " results shown" + (result.has_more ? " · More available" : "") : "No matches. Try fewer words or choose another project.";
    } catch (e) {
      if (!alive || my !== seq) return;
      status.textContent = "Search couldn't load. " + (e.message || "Try again.");
      if (append) more.hidden = false;
    } finally { if (alive && my === seq) { submit.disabled = false; list.removeAttribute("aria-busy"); } }
  }
  shell([{ t: "Home", href: "#/" }, { t: "Search" }], pageHead("Search library", { sub: "Find documents across your projects. Searches stored words in titles and text; all words must match. This does not interpret questions." }), form, status, list, more);
  status.textContent = "Enter words to search all your projects.";
  try {
    var projects = await api("GET", "/api/projects");
    if (!alive) return;
    projects.projects.forEach(function (p) { project.append(h("option", { value: p.id, text: p.title })); });
  } catch (e) { if (alive) status.textContent = "Project filters couldn't load. Search all projects or refresh to retry."; }
  if (alive) query.focus();
}
// ---------- file types: specific labels, Home type filter and list thumbnails (file types) ----------
// Labels come from the head revision's stored MIME type. Thumbnails are made from the revision-pinned content itself, only
// for the image and video types the server serves inline (never SVG, HTML or PDF). Each one is a single request, made only
// while its row is on screen, a few at a time and within a per-minute budget well under the owner's read limit; a 429
// pauses the queue for the server's Retry-After. The bytes are decoded in the browser and kept only as a 96px image for the
// session (content responses are no-store); everything is dropped when the signed-in identity changes. Any failure keeps
// the type icon.
var FILE_TYPE_FILTERS = [["all", "All types"], ["doc", "Docs"], ["page", "Pages"], ["image", "Images"], ["video", "Videos"], ["pdf", "PDFs"], ["csv", "CSV files"], ["json", "JSON files"], ["file", "Other files"]];
var FILE_TYPE_NOUNS = { doc: "docs", page: "pages", image: "images", video: "videos", pdf: "PDFs", csv: "CSV files", json: "JSON files", file: "other files" };
var FILE_MIME_LABELS = { "image/png": "PNG image", "image/jpeg": "JPEG image", "image/webp": "WebP image", "image/gif": "GIF image", "video/mp4": "MP4 video", "video/webm": "WebM video", "application/pdf": "PDF" };
var THUMB_MIME = /^(image\/(png|jpeg|webp|gif)|video\/(mp4|webm))$/;
function fileTypeName(t) { return FILE_TYPE_NOUNS[t] || "items"; }
function typeFilterSelect(value) {
  return h("select", { "aria-label": "Filter by type" }, FILE_TYPE_FILTERS.map(function (x) { return h("option", { value: x[0], selected: value === x[0], text: x[1] }); }));
}
var THUMBS = { gen: 0, active: 0, queue: [], starts: [], timer: 0, pausedUntil: 0, io: null, watched: new Set(), made: new Map(), failed: new Set(), tries: new Map(), waiting: new Map() };
var THUMB_MAX_ACTIVE = 3, THUMB_PER_MIN = 30, THUMB_TIMEOUT = 20000, THUMB_KEEP = 300, THUMB_PX = 96, THUMB_TRIES = 2;
// Larger files keep the type icon instead of being downloaded whole (uploads are currently capped at 5 MiB anyway).
var THUMB_MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1048576;
// Sign-out or a different principal: forget every thumbnail and stop pending work. In-flight work from before is discarded.
function thumbClear() {
  THUMBS.gen++; THUMBS.queue = []; THUMBS.waiting = new Map(); THUMBS.failed = new Set(); THUMBS.tries = new Map(); THUMBS.pausedUntil = 0;
  THUMBS.made.forEach(function (u) { URL.revokeObjectURL(u); }); THUMBS.made = new Map();
  if (THUMBS.io) { THUMBS.io.disconnect(); THUMBS.io = null; }
  THUMBS.watched = new Set();
}
function thumbShow(box, u) {
  var img = h("img", { class: "thumb", src: u, alt: "", decoding: "async" });
  img.addEventListener("load", function () { box.classList.add("has-thumb"); });
  img.addEventListener("error", function () { img.remove(); });
  box.append(img);
}
function thumbForget(box) { if (THUMBS.io) THUMBS.io.unobserve(box); THUMBS.watched.delete(box); box._thumbJob = null; }
// Decodes an image or video Blob and draws a centred, cropped 96px square. cb(objectURL or null); never throws.
function thumbFromBlob(blob, video, cb) {
  var src = URL.createObjectURL(blob), media, called = false;
  function finish(u) { if (called) { if (u) URL.revokeObjectURL(u); return; } called = true; URL.revokeObjectURL(src); if (media) media.removeAttribute("src"); cb(u); }
  function draw(w, hgt) {
    try {
      if (!w || !hgt) return finish(null);
      var c = document.createElement("canvas"), s = Math.max(THUMB_PX / w, THUMB_PX / hgt);
      c.width = THUMB_PX; c.height = THUMB_PX;
      c.getContext("2d").drawImage(media, (THUMB_PX - w * s) / 2, (THUMB_PX - hgt * s) / 2, w * s, hgt * s);
      c.toBlob(function (b) { finish(b ? URL.createObjectURL(b) : null); }, "image/webp", 0.85);
    } catch (e) { finish(null); }
  }
  if (video) {
    media = document.createElement("video");
    media.muted = true; media.playsInline = true; media.preload = "auto";
    media.addEventListener("loadedmetadata", function () {
      var d = isFinite(media.duration) ? media.duration : 0;
      try { media.currentTime = d > 0.2 ? Math.min(0.5, d / 2) : 0.01; } catch (e) { finish(null); }
    });
    media.addEventListener("seeked", function () { draw(media.videoWidth, media.videoHeight); });
  } else {
    media = new Image();
    media.addEventListener("load", function () { draw(media.naturalWidth, media.naturalHeight); });
  }
  media.addEventListener("error", function () { finish(null); });
  media.src = src;
  return function () { finish(null); };
}
function thumbPump() {
  var now = Date.now();
  if (THUMBS.pausedUntil > now) return thumbLater(THUMBS.pausedUntil - now);
  THUMBS.starts = THUMBS.starts.filter(function (t) { return now - t < 60000; });
  while (THUMBS.active < THUMB_MAX_ACTIVE && THUMBS.queue.length) {
    var job = THUMBS.queue[0];
    // Only rows still on the page and on screen; rows scrolled away re-queue when they come back into view.
    var boxes = (THUMBS.waiting.get(job.url) || []).filter(function (b) { return b.isConnected && b._inView !== false; });
    if (!boxes.length) { THUMBS.queue.shift(); THUMBS.waiting.delete(job.url); continue; }
    THUMBS.waiting.set(job.url, boxes);
    if (THUMBS.starts.length >= THUMB_PER_MIN) return thumbLater(60050 - (now - THUMBS.starts[0]));
    THUMBS.queue.shift(); THUMBS.starts.push(now); THUMBS.active++;
    thumbFetch(job);
  }
}
function thumbLater(ms) { if (!THUMBS.timer) THUMBS.timer = setTimeout(function () { THUMBS.timer = 0; thumbPump(); }, Math.max(250, ms)); }
function thumbFetch(job) {
  var gen = THUMBS.gen, finished = false, cancel = null, ctl = "AbortController" in window ? new AbortController() : null;
  var timer = setTimeout(function () { if (ctl) ctl.abort(); if (cancel) cancel(); end(null, "retry"); }, THUMB_TIMEOUT);
  // outcome: "ok", "requeue" (429: back of the pause), "retry" (timeout, network, 5xx: tried again when next on screen,
  // up to THUMB_TRIES times) or "fail" (this revision can't be previewed, including a decode that never finishes).
  function end(u, outcome) {
    if (finished) { if (u) URL.revokeObjectURL(u); return; }
    finished = true; clearTimeout(timer); THUMBS.active--;
    if (gen !== THUMBS.gen) { if (u) URL.revokeObjectURL(u); thumbPump(); return; }
    var boxes = THUMBS.waiting.get(job.url) || [];
    if (outcome === "requeue") { THUMBS.queue.unshift(job); thumbPump(); return; }
    THUMBS.waiting.delete(job.url);
    if (u) {
      THUMBS.made.set(job.url, u);
      if (THUMBS.made.size > THUMB_KEEP) { var old = THUMBS.made.keys().next().value; URL.revokeObjectURL(THUMBS.made.get(old)); THUMBS.made.delete(old); }
      boxes.forEach(function (b) { thumbForget(b); thumbShow(b, u); });
    } else {
      var n = (THUMBS.tries.get(job.url) || 0) + 1; THUMBS.tries.set(job.url, n);
      if (outcome === "fail" || n >= THUMB_TRIES) { THUMBS.failed.add(job.url); boxes.forEach(thumbForget); }
    }
    thumbPump();
  }
  fetch(job.url, { credentials: "same-origin", cache: "no-store", signal: ctl ? ctl.signal : undefined }).then(function (r) {
    if (finished) return;
    if (gen !== THUMBS.gen) return end(null, "retry");
